KERALA GAZETTE

EXTRAORDINARY

PUBLISHED BY AUTHORITY

27th January 2026
Vol. XV No.
13th Makaram 1201
Thiruvananthapuram,

Tuesday
7th Magha 1947

Form A
(See Rule 4)
NOTIFICATION
RDOIJK/1169//2023-B4 19 th January 2026
.......WHEREAS, it is expedient to publish the fair value of land as required under section
28A of the Kerala Stamp Act, 1959 read with rule 3(4) of the Kerala Stamp (Fixation for
Fair Value of Land) Rules, 1995; The fair value of the lands in Edathirinji Village,
Mukundapuram Taluk, Thrissur District, found inevitable to be refixed as on 2025
(including 264% increase from 2010)
NOW, THEREFORE, it is hereby made known to the public that the draft fair value fixed
for the land mentioned against each serial number, in respect of the land situated in the
survey/re-survey numbers of the village and taluk mentioned against each, shall be as
shown against it in column (11) thereof. The objections and suggestions under sub-rule (4)
shall be filed before the Revenue Divisional Officers concerned within a period of sixty
days from the date of notification of the draft fair value in Gazette .

District : Thrissur Taluk : Mukundapuram
Village : Edathirinji Desom : Edathirinji
